HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001","root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2df0d21b8adac462c0a5","text":"3. **Knowledge against public boasting.** Branch evidence: ك ث ر B003, abundance in person/speech/claims; ر ء ي B005, showing off before people; ع ل م B001, disclosure; ع ل م B002, mark that distinguishes. Structural cue: the boasting term stands before, while later sight turns display into exposure. Assigned role: علم distinguishes what was only claimed or performed. Abductive move: I infer that competitive speech/status needs later verification. Reading change: \"you will know\" reads as the collapse of boastful metrics under a stricter mark.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001","root_001040/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_370179ee65b481394c27","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:4:4\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Çoklukla kurulan toplumsal ölçü, mal veya rakip eklenerek büyütülemeyen bir hakikat ölçüsü karşısında geçerliliğini yitirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_39377f892ea9005772f8","text":"The later \"ثم\" in 102:7 and 102:8 retrospectively validated reading the \"ثم\" here as staging rather than emphasis alone. The surprise is that the repeated clause becomes one checkpoint in a chain of escalations: repeated knowledge, then certain knowledge, then seeing, then eye-certainty, then interrogation. 102:8 also made the second warning feel closer to summons than to sermon, because the sequence culminates in passive questioning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3b2dd6818619348de16e","text":"1. **A renewed disclosure separated from the first.** Fixed `تَعْلَمُونَ` again anchors `ع ل م B001` (clarity replacing ignorance), but this occurrence adds initial `ثُمَّ` to the otherwise exact wording of 102:3. The sequence then distinguishes `عِلْمَ ٱلْيَقِينِ`, first sight, and sight with `عَيْنَ ٱلْيَقِينِ`. This second knowing is assigned the role of a later confirmation or escalation. The text supplies only the ordering; I infer that `ثُمَّ` plus exact repetition opens a second epistemic stage rather than merely increasing volume. The fixed ayah therefore reads as promised re-encounter: whatever was known once will return with a separation that prevents the first realization from exhausting it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_501d132e9f470d06f73c","text":"2. **Repetition as boundary reinforcement.** Branch evidence: ع ل م B002, boundary/road marker; ق ب ر B001, cemetery; س ء ل B001, questioning. Structural cue: the same clause is repeated on both sides of \"ثم\", creating a doubled marker before the proof-sequence. Assigned role: علم B002 makes the repeated saying a pair of boundary signs. Abductive move: I infer that repetition marks two thresholds: after the grave-visit and before the certainty/vision phase. Reading change: the ayah becomes a second checkpoint, not rhetorical redundancy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_56c67625c352351a40af","text":"2. **The second warning as a deliberately doubled sign.** Fixed `ع ل م B002` contributes the image of a visible distinguishing mark. Exact repetition makes the fixed occurrence itself structurally conspicuous, and the later movement to `ر ء ي B001` and `ع ي ن B002` turns marking into full manifestation. This second formula's role is to mark the transition twice, like a boundary whose visibility has been reinforced. I supply the signpost analogy and the inference that duplication corrects the possibility of overlooking the first marker. The ayah reads not only as a repeated statement about knowledge but as a formally marked warning whose recurrence performs the very distinguishability latent in `ع ل م`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5fa3e1e3e280eb3badf9","text":"3. **A reversal of the earlier contest.** Fixed `ع ل م B001` includes the lexical scope of competing by knowledge, while contextual `ك ث ر B002` has framed the audience's prior activity as competition by number, wealth, or status; `ي ق ن B001` soon sets settled truth over doubt. The fixed knowing is assigned the role of a counter-metric that cannot be enlarged by adding possessions or rivals. I infer that the surah transfers the arena from socially comparative quantity to noncomparative disclosure; it does not explicitly say that the two are rival metrics. On this activation, `تَعْلَمُونَ` announces the defeat of the counting contest by a reality whose truth is not decided by who has more.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6a11664e214d30a4f561","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:4:4\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Tekrar, sorgudan önce bildirimi yenileyerek bilgisizlik mazeretini daha da daraltır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8d46c68d4dec1a459fdd","text":"1. **A second-stage knowing.** Branch evidence: ع ل م B001, knowing/recognition; ي ق ن B001, settled certainty; ر ء ي B001, sensory seeing; ع ي ن B002, direct eyewitness encounter. Structural cue: the ayah repeats 102:3 but adds \"ثم\", while later ayat add certainty and eye-witnessing. Assigned role: the repeated علم becomes a later layer, not a simple duplicate. Abductive move: I infer that the second warning points to a different phase of disclosure because the packet itself later differentiates علم اليقين from عين اليقين. Reading change: 102:4 reads as staged escalation from impending awareness toward direct verified encounter.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_97aaec858cad900b9d09","text":"102:5–7 confirmed that `ثُمَّ` separates modes of disclosure: settled knowledge is followed by sight and then direct eye-certainty.
